add_WuXun_xp = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = { has_trait = official_seniority }
        }
        add_trait = official_seniority 
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_independent_ruler = no
            NOT = { exists = var:wuxun_xp }
        }
        set_variable = { name = wuxun_xp  value = 0 } 
    }
    add_trait_xp = {
        trait = official_seniority
        track = WuXun
        value = $WuXun_XP$               
    }
    change_variable = {  
        name = wuxun_xp                    
        add = {                           
            value = $WuXun_XP$
            multiply = wuxun_factor
        }
    }
}


add_Wenzhi_xp = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = { has_trait = official_seniority }
        }
        add_trait = official_seniority 
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_independent_ruler = no
            NOT = { exists = var:wenzhi_xp }
        }
        set_variable = { 
            name = wenzhi_xp 
            value = 0 
        } 
    }
    add_trait_xp = {
        trait = official_seniority
        track = WenZhi
        value = $WenZhi_XP$               
    }
    change_variable = {  
        name = wenzhi_xp                    
        add = {                           
            value = $WenZhi_XP$
            multiply = civil_power_calc
        }
    }
}




SanGong_JueWei_CeFeng_effect = {
    add_character_flag = $XUNGUI_JUEWEI$
}

SanGong_add_JueWei_effect = {
    save_scope_as = XunChen
    hidden_effect = {
        SanGong_remove_JueWei_effect = yes
        add_trait = $XUNJUE$
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            top_liege.dynasty = {
                has_variable = han_juewei
            }
        }
        hidden_effect = {
            add_trait_xp = { trait = $XUNJUE$ track = Juewei_ChaoDai_name value = -100 }
        }
    }
	else_if = {
        limit = {
            top_liege.dynasty = {
                has_variable = ming_juewei
            }
        }
        hidden_effect = {
            add_trait_xp = { trait = $XUNJUE$ track = Juewei_ChaoDai_name value = 100 }   
        }
    }
    else = {
        hidden_effect = {
            add_trait_xp = { trait = $XUNJUE$ track = Juewei_ChaoDai_name value = -100 }
            add_trait_xp = { trait = $XUNJUE$ track = Juewei_ChaoDai_name value = 50 }
        }
    }
    
    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:XunChen = {
                is_independent_ruler = no
                has_trait = SanGong_Juewei_GongJue
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen = {
            create_character_memory = { 
                type = mem_receive_hereditary_title_4 
                participants = {
                    owner = scope:XunChen
                }
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen.top_liege = {
            create_character_memory = { 
                type = mem_receive_hereditary_title_4 
                participants = {
                    owner = scope:XunChen
                }
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen.top_liege = {
            trigger_event = SanGong_hidden.008
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            scope:XunChen = {
                is_independent_ruler = no
                has_trait = SanGong_Juewei_HouJue
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen = {
            create_character_memory = { 
                type = mem_receive_hereditary_title_3
                participants = {
                    owner = scope:XunChen
                }
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen.top_liege = {
            create_character_memory = { 
                type = mem_receive_hereditary_title_3
                participants = {
                    owner = scope:XunChen
                }
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen.top_liege = {
            trigger_event = SanGong_hidden.007
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            scope:XunChen = {
                is_independent_ruler = no
                has_trait = SanGong_Juewei_BoJue
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen = {
            create_character_memory = { 
                type = mem_receive_hereditary_title_2
                participants = {
                    owner = scope:XunChen
                }
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen.top_liege = {
            create_character_memory = { 
                type = mem_receive_hereditary_title_2 
                participants = {
                    owner = scope:XunChen
                }
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen.top_liege = {
            trigger_event = SanGong_hidden.006
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            scope:XunChen = {
                is_independent_ruler = no
                has_trait = SanGong_Juewei_ZiJue
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen = {
            create_character_memory = { 
                type = mem_receive_hereditary_title_1
                participants = {
                    owner = scope:XunChen
                }
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen.top_liege = {
            create_character_memory = { 
                type = mem_receive_hereditary_title_1 
                participants = {
                    owner = scope:XunChen
                }
            }
        }
        scope:XunChen.top_liege = {
            trigger_event = SanGong_JueWei_event.040
        }
    }
}

SanGong_FengJue_interaction_effect = {
    scope:recipient = {
        save_scope_as = XunChen
        custom_tooltip = SanGong_FengJue_effect_GongXun_tt
    }
    scope:actor = {
        remove_treasury_or_gold = 100
        add_prestige = 50
        change_influence = 50
        add_legitimacy = 50
    }
}






create_battle_won_memory_effect = {
    # 保存战斗地点
    combat.location = { save_scope_as = battle_province }

    # 创建记忆（scope:new_memory 自动指向新创建的记忆）
    create_character_memory = {
        type = $BATTLE_MEMORY$
        participants = {
            loser = root.enemy_side.side_commander
            ruler = root.enemy_side.side_primary_participant
        }
    }

    # 把新记忆保存到命名作用域
    scope:new_memory = { save_scope_as = battle_memory }

    # 将战斗地点存入记忆的变量
    scope:battle_memory = {
        set_variable = {
            name = battle_location
            value = scope:battle_province
        }
    }
}

SanGong_JueWei_rank_inherit_effect = {#爵位等级继承
    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:Xungui_death = {
                has_trait = SanGong_Juewei_GongJue
            }
        }
        create_character_memory = { 
            type = mem_inherit_ancestral_title_4
            participants = {
                xungui = scope:Xungui_death
            }
        }
        add_trait = SanGong_Juewei_GongJue
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            scope:Xungui_death = {
                has_trait = SanGong_Juewei_HouJue
            }
        }
        create_character_memory = { 
            type = mem_inherit_ancestral_title_3
            participants = {
                xungui = scope:Xungui_death
            }
        }
        add_trait = SanGong_Juewei_HouJue
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            scope:Xungui_death = {
                has_trait = SanGong_Juewei_BoJue
            }
        }
        create_character_memory = { 
            type = mem_inherit_ancestral_title_2 
            participants = {
                xungui = scope:Xungui_death
            }
        }
        add_trait = SanGong_Juewei_BoJue
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            scope:Xungui_death = {
                has_trait = SanGong_Juewei_ZiJue
            }
        }
        create_character_memory = { 
            type = mem_inherit_ancestral_title_1 
            participants = {
                xungui = scope:Xungui_death
            }
        }
        add_trait = SanGong_Juewei_ZiJue
    }
}

SanGong_JueWei_inherit_all_effect = {#爵位全部继承
    remove_character_flag = ZhiDingXiJue_flag
    SanGong_JueWei_rank_inherit_effect = yes
    SanGong_Juewei_address_effect = yes
    scope:Xungui_death = {
        SanGong_JueWei_inherit_effect = yes
    }
    top_liege = {
        trigger_event = {
            id = SanGong_hidden.009
            days = 1
        }
    }
}

Xungui_death_inherit_effect = {# 勋贵赐死/亡故后的爵位继承核心效果
    # 目标：离世的勋贵角色
    scope:Xungui_death = {
    
        # ==============================================
        # 继承优先级 1：有【指定袭爵标记】的家族成员（优先继承）
        # ==============================================
        if = {
            # 判定条件：存在符合条件的【指定继承人】
            limit = {
                house ?= {
				    any_house_member = {
                        house = scope:Xungui_death.house       # 同王朝
                        top_liege = scope:xungui_liege             # 同君主
                        is_male = yes                               # 男性
                        is_alive = yes                               # 存活
                        has_character_flag = ZhiDingXiJue_flag       # 拥有【指定袭爵】标记
                    }
                }
            }
            house ?= {
                every_house_member = {
                    limit = {
                        dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                        top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                        is_male = yes
                        is_alive = yes
                        has_character_flag = ZhiDingXiJue_flag
                    }
                    save_scope_as = Xungui                         # 保存为继承人 scope
                    SanGong_JueWei_inherit_all_effect = yes         # 执行全套爵位继承
                }
            }

        }
        
        # ==============================================
        # 继承优先级 2：无指定继承人 → 【亲生儿子】继承（长子优先）
        # ==============================================
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                any_close_family_member = {
                    dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                    top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                    is_male = yes
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_child_of = scope:Xungui_death            # 是死者亲生儿子
                    NOT = { has_trait = incapable }             # 非无能/残废
                }
            }
            
            # 按年龄排序 → 取长子
            ordered_close_family_member = {
                limit = {
                    dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                    top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                    is_male = yes
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_child_of = scope:Xungui_death
                    NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
                }
                order_by = age                                  # 按年龄排序
                max = 1                                         # 只取最年长1人
                save_scope_as = Xungui
                SanGong_JueWei_inherit_all_effect = yes
            }
        }
        
        # ==============================================
        # 继承优先级 3：无亲生儿子 → 【长孙】继承
        # ==============================================
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                # 无儿子
                NOT = {
                    any_close_family_member = {
                        dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                        top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                        is_male = yes
                        is_alive = yes
                        is_child_of = scope:Xungui_death
                    }
                }
                # 但有孙子
                any_close_family_member = {
                    dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                    top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                    is_male = yes
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_grandchild_of = scope:Xungui_death        # 是死者孙子
                    NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
                }
            }
            
            # 取最年长的孙子
            ordered_close_family_member = {
                limit = {
                    dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                    top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                    is_male = yes
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_grandchild_of = scope:Xungui_death
                    NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
                }
                order_by = age
                max = 1
                save_scope_as = Xungui
                SanGong_JueWei_inherit_all_effect = yes
            }
        }
        
        # ==============================================
        # 继承优先级 4：无儿孙 → 【曾孙】继承
        # ==============================================
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                # 无儿子
                NOT = {
                    any_close_family_member = {
                        dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                        top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                        is_male = yes
                        is_alive = yes
                        is_child_of = scope:Xungui_death
                    }
                }
                # 无孙子
                NOT = {
                    any_close_family_member = {
                        dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                        top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                        is_male = yes
                        is_alive = yes
                        is_grandchild_of = scope:Xungui_death
                    }
                }
                # 有曾孙
                any_close_family_member = {
                    dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                    top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                    is_male = yes
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_great_grandchild_of = scope:Xungui_death  # 是死者曾孙
                    NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
                }
            }
            
            # 取最年长的曾孙
            ordered_close_family_member = {
                limit = {
                    dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                    top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                    is_male = yes
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_great_grandchild_of = scope:Xungui_death
                    NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
                }
                order_by = age
                max = 1
                save_scope_as = Xungui
                SanGong_JueWei_inherit_all_effect = yes
            }
        }
        
        # ==============================================
        # 继承优先级 5：无直系后代 → 【同族近亲】继承
        # ==============================================
        else_if = {
            # 选取同王朝、同君主、存活男性
            ordered_close_family_member = {
                limit = {
                    dynasty = scope:Xungui_death.dynasty
                    top_liege = scope:xungui_liege
                    is_male = yes
                    is_alive = yes
                }
                order_by = age                                  # 年长者优先
                max = 1
                save_scope_as = Xungui
                SanGong_JueWei_inherit_all_effect = yes
            }
        }
    }
}

Xungui_death_not_inherit_effect = {
    top_liege = {
        create_character = {
            age = 16
            gender_female_chance = 100
            culture = root.culture
            faith = root.faith
            dynasty = generate
            random_traits = yes
            save_scope_as = Xungui
        }
    }
    scope:Xungui_death = {
        SanGong_JueWei_inherit_effect = yes
    }
    top_liege = {
        trigger_event = {
            id = SanGong_hidden.010
            days = 1
        }
    }
}


SiziXijue_effect = {#嗣子承袭爵位
    if = {
        limit = {
            $SIZI$ = {
                is_ruler = no
            }
        }
        add_courtier = $SIZI$
    }
    $SIZI$ = { 
        if = {
            limit = { exists = father }
            father = { save_scope_as = SanGong_ZongShi_previous_father }
        }
            limit = {
                NOT = {
                    is_child_of = $TARGET$
                    is_grandchild_of = $TARGET$
                    is_great_grandchild_of = $TARGET$
                }
            }
        set_father = $TARGET$
        SanGong_ZongShi_reconcile_parent_change_effect = yes
    }
}


PaiXizhuanhuan_effect = {
	# 霸主自动拉人进【亲霸主派系】
	if = {
		limit = { scope:actor = title:h_china.holder }
		situation:dynastic_cycle ?= {
			situation_participant_group:pro_hegemon_movement = { save_scope_as = actors_movement }
		}
	}
	else = {
		situation:dynastic_cycle ?= {
			random_participant_group = {
				limit = { participant_group_has_character = scope:actor }
				save_scope_as = actors_movement
			}
		}

		# 发送通知 + 派系转换
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = celestial_ask_to_switch_movements_interaction_accept_tt
				left_icon = scope:recipient
				
				scope:actors_movement = {
					switch = {
						trigger = participant_group_type
						
						# 加入亲皇派
						pro_hegemon_movement = {
							scope:recipient = {
								custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_pro_hegemon_tt
								custom_tooltip = adds_movement_power_tt
								
								every_relation = {
									type = disciple
									custom = every_disciple_joins_movement
									custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_pro_hegemon_tt
									custom_tooltip = every_disciple_adds_movement_power_tt
									
									if = {
										limit = { is_ai = yes }
										set_variable = { name = movement_member value = flag:pro_hegemon }
										set_variable = { name = persuaded_to_join_movement value = yes }
									}
									else = {
										send_interface_message = {
											type = send_interface_message
											title = celestial_ask_to_switch_movements_interaction_accept_tt
											left_icon = scope:recipient
											custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_pro_hegemon_tt
										}
									}
								}
								
								set_variable = { name = movement_member value = flag:pro_hegemon }
								set_variable = { name = persuaded_to_join_movement value = yes }
							}
						}
						
						# 加入开拓派
						expansion_movement = {
							scope:recipient = {
								custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_expansion_tt
								custom_tooltip = adds_movement_power_tt
								
								every_relation = {
									type = disciple
									custom = every_disciple_joins_movement
									custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_expansion_tt
									custom_tooltip = every_disciple_adds_movement_power_tt
									
									if = {
										limit = { is_ai = yes }
										set_variable = { name = movement_member value = flag:expansion }
										set_variable = { name = persuaded_to_join_movement value = yes }
									}
									else = {
										send_interface_message = {
											type = send_interface_message
											title = celestial_ask_to_switch_movements_interaction_accept_tt
											left_icon = scope:recipient
											custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_expansion_tt
										}
									}
								}
								
								set_variable = { name = movement_member value = flag:expansion }
								set_variable = { name = persuaded_to_join_movement value = yes }
							}
						}
						
						# 加入进取派
						advancement_movement = {
							scope:recipient = {
								custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_advancement_tt
								custom_tooltip = adds_movement_power_tt
								
								every_relation = {
									type = disciple
									custom = every_disciple_joins_movement
									custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_advancement_tt
									custom_tooltip = every_disciple_adds_movement_power_tt
									
									if = {
										limit = { is_ai = yes }
										set_variable = { name = movement_member value = flag:advancement }
										set_variable = { name = persuaded_to_join_movement value = yes }
									}
									else = {
										send_interface_message = {
											type = send_interface_message
											title = celestial_ask_to_switch_movements_interaction_accept_tt
											left_icon = scope:recipient
											custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_advancement_tt
										}
									}
								}
								
								set_variable = { name = movement_member value = flag:advancement }
								set_variable = { name = persuaded_to_join_movement value = yes }
							}
						}
						
						# 加入保守派
						conservative_movement = {
							scope:recipient = {
								custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_conservative_tt
								custom_tooltip = adds_movement_power_tt
								
								every_relation = {
									type = disciple
									custom = every_disciple_joins_movement
									custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_conservative_tt
									custom_tooltip = every_disciple_adds_movement_power_tt
									
									if = {
										limit = { is_ai = yes }
										set_variable = { name = movement_member value = flag:conservative }
										set_variable = { name = persuaded_to_join_movement value = yes }
									}
									else = {
										send_interface_message = {
											type = send_interface_message
											title = celestial_ask_to_switch_movements_interaction_accept_tt
											left_icon = scope:recipient
											custom_tooltip = celestial_switch_movement_to_conservative_tt
										}
									}
								}
								
								set_variable = { name = movement_member value = flag:conservative }
								set_variable = { name = persuaded_to_join_movement value = yes }
							}
						}
					}
				}

				# 获得功德
				change_merit = {
					value = miniscule_merit_gain
					add = scope:recipient.number_of_disciples
				}


				# 建立师徒/长辈关系
				set_elder_relation_effect = { ELDER = scope:actor DISCIPLE = scope:recipient MERIT = minor_merit_gain }
			}
		}
	}

	# 目标与门徒刷新派系归属
	scope:recipient = {
		hidden_effect = {
			recalculate_participant_group = situation:dynastic_cycle
			every_relation = {
				type = disciple
				recalculate_participant_group = situation:dynastic_cycle
			}
		}
	}
}


SG_set_liuguan_effect = {
    random_vassal_or_below = {
        limit = {
            target_is_liege_or_above = scope:HuangDi
            highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
            is_male = yes  
            age <= 70        
            is_adult = yes             
            is_healthy = yes       
            is_imprisoned = no 
            merit_level >= 4
        }
        alternative_limit = {
            target_is_liege_or_above = scope:HuangDi
            highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
            is_adult = yes                       
            is_healthy = yes                  
            is_imprisoned = no    
            merit_level >= 2           
        }
        save_scope_as = LiuGuan
    }
}
